**IP路由基础 \-- IP路由基础配置命令 \-- display ip routing-table**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[display ip routing-table]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1052034451}[命令用来显示路由表的信息。]{style="font-family:
宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1624130382}

[**[display ip routing-table]{lang="EN-US"}**[ \[ **topology** *topo-name* \| **vpn-instance** *vpn-instance-name* \] \[ **verbose** \]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_2076487939}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_76141950}

[[任意视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_474894964}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_917799086}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1499114502}

[[network-operator]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x654769690}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1676139793}

[[mdc-operator]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1399119757}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1796692788}

[**[topology]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *topo-name*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1335653505}[：显示指定拓扑的信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[topo-name]{lang="EN-US"}*[表示拓扑名，为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[31]{lang="EN-US"}[个字符的字符串，区分大小写；]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[base]{lang="EN-US"}**[为公网拓扑。如果未指定本参数，则显示公网的信息。本参数的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[vpn-instance ]{lang="EN-US"}***[vpn-instance-name]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_828331864}[：显示指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[的信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vpn-instance-name]{lang="EN-US"}*[表示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS L3VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[实例名称，为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[31]{lang="EN-US"}[个字符的字符串，区分大小写。如果未指定本参数，则显示公网的信息。本参数的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[verbose]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1352012664}[：显示全部路由表的详细信息，包括激活路由和未激活路由。如果未指定本参数，将显示激活路由的概要信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1540286602}

**IP路由基础 \-- IP路由基础配置命令 \-- display ip routing-table acl**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[display ip routing-table acl]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x2078082897}[命令用来显示通过指定]{style="font-family:
宋体"}[ACL]{lang="EN-US"}[过滤的路由信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1636174987}

[**[display ip routing-table ]{lang="EN-US"}**[\[ **topology** *topo-name* \| **vpn-instance** *vpn-instance-name* \] **acl** *acl-number* \[ **verbose** \]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1082442079}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_217994671}

[[任意视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1652170018}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_917733553}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_2076487942}

[[network-operator]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x2028703731}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1091119737}

[[mdc-operator]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1711456486}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1114723800}

[**[topology]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *topo-name*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1036737342}[：显示指定拓扑的信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[topo-name]{lang="EN-US"}*[表示拓扑名，为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[31]{lang="EN-US"}[个字符的字符串，区分大小写；]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[base]{lang="EN-US"}**[为公网拓扑。如果未指定本参数，则显示公网的信息。本参数的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[vpn-instance ]{lang="EN-US"}***[vpn-instance-name]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x37443853}[：显示指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[的信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vpn-instance-name]{lang="EN-US"}*[表示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS L3VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[实例名称，为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[31]{lang="EN-US"}[个字符的字符串，区分大小写。如果未指定本参数，则显示公网的信息。本参数的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[acl-number]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1462936152}[：基本]{style="font-family:宋体"}[ACL]{lang="EN-US"}[的编号，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[2000]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[2999]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[verbose]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_666777586}[：显示通过指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[ACL]{lang="EN-US"}[过滤的所有路由的详细信息。如果未指定本参数，将只显示通过指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[ACL]{lang="EN-US"}[过滤的激活路由的概要信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1335171943}

[[如果用户指定的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[ACL]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1157577016}[不存在或者]{style="font-family:宋体"}[ACL]{lang="EN-US"}[中没有任何规则，将显示所有的路由信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x361958350}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_917864625}[配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[ACL 2000]{lang="EN-US"}[，并设置路由过滤规则。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

**IP路由基础 \-- IP路由基础配置命令 \-- display ip routing-table ip-address**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[display ip routing-table ]{lang="EN-US"}***[ip-address]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_607173836}[命令用来显示指定目的地址的路由信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[display ip routing-table ]{lang="EN-US"}***[ip-address1 ]{lang="EN-US"}***[to]{lang="EN-US"}***[ ip-address2]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1622314591}[命令用来显示指定目的地址范围内的路由信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x639013532}

[**[display ip routing-table]{lang="EN-US"}**[ \[ **topology** *topo-name* \| **vpn-instance** *vpn-instance-name* \] *ip-address* \[ *mask* \| *mask-length* \] \[ **longer-match** \] \[ **verbose** \]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_817201403}

[**[display ip routing-table]{lang="EN-US"}**[ \[ **topology** *topo-name* \| **vpn-instance** *vpn-instance-name* \] *ip-address1* **to** *ip-address2* \[ **verbose** \]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x2005131013}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1634765143}

[[任意视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x2119512658}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1356829910}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1091290434}

[[network-operator]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x2017969613}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1095192793}

[[mdc-operator]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x2036240559}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_685024593}

[**[topology]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *topo-name*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1036606272}[：显示指定拓扑的信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[topo-name]{lang="EN-US"}*[表示拓扑名，为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[31]{lang="EN-US"}[个字符的字符串，区分大小写；]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[base]{lang="EN-US"}**[为公网拓扑。如果未指定本参数，则显示公网的信息。本参数的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[vpn-instance ]{lang="EN-US"}***[vpn-instance-name]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1811149807}[：显示指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[的信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vpn-instance-name]{lang="EN-US"}*[表示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS L3VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[实例名称，为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[31]{lang="EN-US"}[个字符的字符串，区分大小写。如果未指定本参数，则显示公网的信息。本参数的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[ip-address]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1940197309}[：目的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[地址，点分十进制格式。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[mask/mask-length]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1501632867}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[地址掩码，点分十进制格式或以整数形式表示的长度，当用整数时，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[32]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[longer-match]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x138632543}[：匹配掩码更长的路由。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[ip-address1]{lang="EN-US"}*[ **to** *ip-address2*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_107400207}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[地址范围。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[ip-address1]{lang="EN-US"}*[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[ip-address2]{lang="EN-US"}*[共同决定一个地址范围，只有地址在此范围内的路由才会被显示。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[verbose]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1606753858}[：显示全部路由表的详细信息，包括激活路由和未激活路由。如果未指定本参数，将显示激活路由的概要信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x492483893}

[[使用不同的可选参数，命令的输出也不相同，以下是对该命令不同形式的输出说明：]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_2145175643}

[[(1)[      ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US"}**[display ip routing-table]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *ip-address*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1539103007}

[[显示满足如下条件的所有激活路由：]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_599951766}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[用户输入的目的]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1811018735}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[地址同路由表中各条路由的子网掩码值进行与运算；]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[路由表中各条路由的目的]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_869507101}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[地址同其自身子网掩码值进行与运算；]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[两次运算结果相同的路由条目将被显示出来。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1487207833}

[[(2)[      ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US"}**[display ip routing-table]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *ip-address* *mask*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_141546882}

[[显示满足如下条件的所有激活路由：]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_620669307}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[用户输入的目的]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1662947487}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[地址同用户输入的子网掩码值进行与运算；]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[路由表中各条路由的目的]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x386235671}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[地址同用户输入的子网掩码值进行与运算；]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[两次运算结果相同，并且掩码小于等于用户输入的子网掩码的路由条目将被显示出来。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_60394312}

[[(3)[      ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US"}**[display ip routing-table]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *ip-address* **longer-match**]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x87453734}

[[显示满足如下条件的所有激活路由：]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x635614869}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[用户输入的目的]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1810428911}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[地址同路由表中各条路由的子网掩码值进行与运算；]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[路由表中各条路由的目的]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1002149821}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[地址同其自身子网掩码值进行与运算；]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[两次运算结果相同，并且子网掩码最长匹配的路由条目将被显示出来。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1269382460}

[[(4)[      ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US"}**[display ip routing-table]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *ip-address mask* **longer-match**]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1031093539}

[[显示满足如下条件的所有激活路由：]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_867010999}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[用户输入的目的]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1499346244}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[地址同用户输入的子网掩码值进行与运算；]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[路由表中各条路由的目的]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1823194622}[IP]{lang="EN-US"}[地址同用户输入的子网掩码值进行与运算；]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[两次运算结果相同，掩码小于等于用户输入的子网掩码，同时子网掩码最长匹配的路由条目将被显示出来。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_35378445}

[[(5)[      ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US"}**[display ip routing-table]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *ip-address1* **to** *ip-address2*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_512373416}

[[显示]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[ip-address1]{lang="EN-US"}*[/32]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x356036331}[到]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[ip-address2]{lang="EN-US"}*[/32]{lang="EN-US"}[之间的激活路由，目的地址与掩码（]{style="font-family:宋体"}[32]{lang="EN-US"}[位）同时在指定范围内才会显示。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

**IP路由基础 \-- IP路由基础配置命令 \-- display ip routing-table prefix-list**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[display ip routing-table prefix-list]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_2107995817}[命令用来显示通过指定前缀列表过滤的路由信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1197318874}

[**[display ip routing-table ]{lang="EN-US"}**[\[ **topology** *topo-name* \| **vpn-instance** *vpn-instance-name* \] **prefix-list** *prefix-list-name* \[ **verbose** \]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x825413875}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1958119961}

[[任意视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x916695759}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_653668531}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1569673369}

[[network-operator]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1811215342}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1094118212}

[[mdc-operator]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x807934950}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1239301072}

[**[topology]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *topo-name*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1036606271}[：显示指定拓扑的信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[topo-name]{lang="EN-US"}*[表示拓扑名，为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[31]{lang="EN-US"}[个字符的字符串，区分大小写；]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[base]{lang="EN-US"}**[为公网拓扑。如果未指定本参数，则显示公网的信息。本参数的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[vpn-instance ]{lang="EN-US"}***[vpn-instance-name]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1789410184}[：显示指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[的信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vpn-instance-name]{lang="EN-US"}*[表示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS L3VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[实例名称，为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[31]{lang="EN-US"}[个字符的字符串，区分大小写。如果未指定本参数，则显示公网的信息。本参数的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[prefix-list-name]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1830470887}[：前缀列表名称，为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[63]{lang="EN-US"}[个字符的字符串，区分大小写。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[verbose]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x562555745}[：当使用该参数时，显示通过过滤规则的所有路由的详细信息。如果未指定本参数，将只显示通过过滤规则的激活路由的概要信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x232562954}

[[如果指定的前缀列表不存在，将显示所有的路由信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1726231633}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1220249878}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1811084270}[配置地址前缀列表]{style="font-family:宋体"}[test]{lang="EN-US"}[允许前缀为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1.1.1.0]{lang="EN-US"}[，掩码长度为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[24]{lang="EN-US"}[的路由通过。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[display ip routing-table]{lang="EN-US"}**[ **protocol**]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x563934120}[命令用来显示指定协议生成或发现的路由信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x850988010}

[**[display ip routing-table]{lang="EN-US"}**[ \[ **topology** *topo-name* \| **vpn-instance** *vpn-instance-name* \] **protocol** *protocol* \[ **inactive** \| **verbose** \]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_401280620}

[[mdc-operator]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_300193472}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x162141009}

[**[topology]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *topo-name*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1036737346}[：显示指定拓扑的信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[topo-name]{lang="EN-US"}*[表示拓扑名，为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[31]{lang="EN-US"}[个字符的字符串，区分大小写；]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[base]{lang="EN-US"}**[为公网拓扑。如果未指定本参数，则显示公网的信息。本参数的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[vpn-instance ]{lang="EN-US"}***[vpn-instance-name]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1386386780}[：显示指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[的信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vpn-instance-name]{lang="EN-US"}*[表示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS L3VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[实例名称，为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[31]{lang="EN-US"}[个字符的字符串，区分大小写。如果未指定本参数，则显示公网的信息。本参数的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[protocol]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1499176765}[：显示指定路由协议的信息，包括]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[bgp]{lang="EN-US"}**[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[direct]{lang="EN-US"}**[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[isis]{lang="EN-US"}**[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[ospf]{lang="EN-US"}**[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[rip]{lang="EN-US"}**[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[static]{lang="EN-US"}**[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[inactive]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1490902375}[：显示未激活路由的信息。如果未指定本参数，则显示激活路由和未激活路由的信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[verbose]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x474517933}[：当使用该参数时，显示路由的详细信息。如果未指定本参数，将显示路由的概要信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

宋体"}[IPv6 ACL]{lang="EN-US"}[过滤的]{style="font-family:
宋体"}[IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}[路由信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1651942168}

[**[display ipv6 routing-table ]{lang="EN-US"}**[\[ **vpn-instance** *vpn-instance-name* \] **acl** *acl-number* \[ **verbose** \]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_551214040}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1927403523}

[**[vpn-instance]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *vpn-instance-name*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x527716005}[：显示指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[的信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vpn-instance-name]{lang="EN-US"}*[表示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS L3VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[实例名称，为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[31]{lang="EN-US"}[个字符的字符串，区分大小写。如果未指定本参数，则显示公网的信息。本参数的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[acl6-number]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x905177020}[：基本]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv6 ACL]{lang="EN-US"}[编号，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[2000]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[2999]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[verbose]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_656297646}[：显示通过指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv6 ACL]{lang="EN-US"}[过滤的所有路由的详细信息。如果未指定本参数，只显示通过]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv6 ACL]{lang="EN-US"}[过滤的激活路由的概要信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_623530618}

[[如果指定的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv6 ACL]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1851585083}[不存在或者]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv6 ACL]{lang="EN-US"}[中没有任何规则，将显示所有的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}[路由信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

**IP路由基础 \-- IP路由基础配置命令 \-- display ipv6 routing-table ipv6-address**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[display ipv6 routing-table ]{lang="EN-US"}***[ipv6-address]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x617741601}[命令用来显示指定目的地址的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}[路由信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[display ipv6 routing-table ]{lang="EN-US"}***[ipv6-address1 ]{lang="EN-US"}***[to]{lang="EN-US"}***[ ipv6-address2]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1949671900}[命令用来显示指定目的地址范围内的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}[路由信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x270792833}

[**[display ipv6 routing-table]{lang="EN-US"}**[ \[ **vpn-instance** *vpn-instance-name* \] *ipv6-address* \[ *prefix-length* \] \[ **longer-match** \] \[ **verbose** \]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_545816588}

[**[display ipv6 routing-table ]{lang="EN-US"}**[\[ **vpn-instance** *vpn-instance-name* \] *ipv6-address1* **to** *ipv6-address2* \[ **verbose** \]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_2080820463}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1035053448}

[[任意视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x2136979385}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1043230943}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1590551855}

[[network-operator]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_265911168}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1161124965}

[[mdc-operator]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_2117232441}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_206507816}

[**[vpn-instance]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *vpn-instance-name*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1513267833}[：显示指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[的信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vpn-instance-name]{lang="EN-US"}*[表示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS L3VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[实例名称，为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[31]{lang="EN-US"}[个字符的字符串，区分大小写。如果未指定本参数，则显示公网的信息。本参数的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[ipv6-address]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1767212544}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}[目的地址。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[prefix-length]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1390711908}[：前缀长度，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[128]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[longer-match]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1034806193}[：匹配并显示前缀最长的路由条目。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[ipv6-address1]{lang="EN-US"}*[ **to** *ipv6-address2*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x2107561244}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}[地址范围。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[ipv6-address1]{lang="EN-US"}*[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[ipv6-address2]{lang="EN-US"}*[共同决定一个地址范围，只有地址在此范围内的路由才会被显示。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[verbose]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1665567157}[：显示激活和未激活路由的详细信息。如果未指定本参数，将显示激活路由的概要信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x568092113}

[[使用不同的可选参数，命令的输出也不相同，以下是对该命令不同形式的输出说明：]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1301280362}

[[(1)[      ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US"}**[display ipv6 routing-table]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *ipv6-address*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1009215346}

[[显示满足如下条件的所有激活路由：]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x754970096}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[用户输入的目的]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_2117297977}[IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}[地址同路由表中各条路由的前缀长度值进行与运算；]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[路由表中各条路由的目的]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x262640392}[IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}[地址同其自身前缀长度值进行与运算；]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[两次运算结果相同的路由条目将被显示出来。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1445077389}

[[(2)[      ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US"}**[display ipv6 routing-table]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *ipv6-address* *prefix-length*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1504967879}

[[显示满足如下条件的所有激活路由：]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1869709464}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[用户输入的目的]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1398267084}[IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}[地址同用户输入的前缀长度值进行与运算；]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[路由表中各条路由的目的]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_735171590}[IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}[地址同用户输入的前缀长度值进行与运算；]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[两次运算结果相同，并且路由表中前缀长度小于等于用户输入的前缀长度的路由条目将被显示出来。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_287491007}

[[(3)[      ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US"}**[display ipv6 routing-table]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *ipv6-address* **longer-match**]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_947857206}

[[显示满足如下条件的所有激活路由：]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_2117494585}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[用户输入的目的]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1334889356}[IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}[地址同路由表中各条路由的前缀长度值进行与运算；]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[·[              ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US" style="font-size:10.0pt;font-family:Symbol"}[路由表中各条路由的目的]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_225985619}[IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}[地址同其自身前缀长度值进行与运算；]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[(4)[      ]{style="font:7.0pt "}]{lang="EN-US"}**[display ipv6 routing-table]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *ipv6-address prefix-length* **longer-match**]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x586990467}

[**[vpn-instance]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *vpn-instance-name*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_285924352}[：显示指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[的信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vpn-instance-name]{lang="EN-US"}*[表示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS L3VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[实例名称，为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[31]{lang="EN-US"}[个字符的字符串，区分大小写。如果未指定本参数，则显示公网的信息。本参数的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[prefix-list-name]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_169196155}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}[前缀列表的名称，为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[63]{lang="EN-US"}[个字符的字符串，区分大小写。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[verbose]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_2141119213}[：显示所有路由的详细信息。如果未指定本参数，只显示激活路由的概要信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

**IP路由基础 \-- IP路由基础配置命令 \-- display ipv6 routing-table protocol**

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[display ipv6 routing-table protocol]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x17432812}[命令用来显示指定协议生成或发现的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}[路由信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x2074059448}

[**[display ipv6 routing-table]{lang="EN-US"}**[ \[ **vpn-instance** *vpn-instance-name* \] **protocol** *protocol* \[ **inactive** \| **verbose** \]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_981238689}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_2117363515}

[[任意视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x567961041}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1853385153}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x689382141}

[[network-operator]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_240868206}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x612749705}

[[mdc-operator]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_190579452}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1829697015}

[**[vpn-instance]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *vpn-instance-name*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_2117297979}[：显示指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[的信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vpn-instance-name]{lang="EN-US"}*[表示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS L3VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[实例名称，为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[31]{lang="EN-US"}[个字符的字符串，区分大小写。如果未指定本参数，则显示公网的信息。本参数的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[protocol]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x262509320}[：显示指定路由协议的信息，包括]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[bgp4+]{lang="EN-US"}**[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[direct]{lang="EN-US"}**[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[isisv6]{lang="EN-US"}**[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[ospfv3]{lang="EN-US"}**[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[ripng]{lang="EN-US"}**[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[static]{lang="EN-US"}**[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[inactive]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x468717338}[：如果配置了该参数，此命令只显示未激活路由信息。如果未指定本参数，将显示所有激活和未激活路由信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[verbose]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x695381593}[：显示激活和未激活路由的详细信息。如果未指定本参数，将显示路由的概要信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[本命令的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:KaiTi_GB2312"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x749586949}
:::

**[ ]{lang="EN-US"}**

[**[display rib nib]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_451433872}[命令用来显示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[RIB]{lang="EN-US"}[的下一跳信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x419916584}

[**[display rib nib ]{lang="EN-US"}**[\[ **self-originated** \] \[ *nib-id* \] \[ **verbose** \]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x611257696}

[**[display rib nib protocol ]{lang="EN-US"}***[protocol-name]{lang="EN-US"}*[ \[ **verbose** \]]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x611323232}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x611519839}

[[任意视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1505821371}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1055823747}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_721077820}

[[network-operator]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_584355762}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_56618030}

[[mdc-operator]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x611585375}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1269223341}

[**[self-originated]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_115517145}[：路由管理自己生成的下一跳信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[nib-id]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x611388767}[：路由下一跳信息的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[ID]{lang="EN-US"}[值，取值范围]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[FFFFFFFF]{lang="EN-US"}[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[verbose]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1274251475}[：显示详细信息。如果未指定本参数，则显示概要信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[protocol ]{lang="EN-US"}***[protocol-name]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_820963760}[：显示指定路由协议生成的下一跳信息，包括]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[bgp]{lang="EN-US"}**[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[direct]{lang="EN-US"}**[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[isis]{lang="EN-US"}**[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[ospf]{lang="EN-US"}**[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[rip]{lang="EN-US"}**[和]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[static]{lang="EN-US"}**[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x827177330}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_95587385}[显示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[RIB]{lang="EN-US"}[的下一跳信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

margin-left:31.2pt;margin-right:0cm"}
[[本命令的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:KaiTi_GB2312"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_257443086}
:::

[ ]{lang="EN-US"}

[**[ecmp mode enhanced]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1445859341}[命令用来使能]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv4]{lang="EN-US"}[等价路由增强模式功能。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo ecmp mode]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1059478597}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1445924877}

[**[ecmp mode enhanced]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_359493702}

[**[undo ecmp mode]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1445990413}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_669532848}

[[IPv4]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1446580237}[等价路由增强模式功能处于关闭状态。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x233946318}

[[系统视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1446645773}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x989796932}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1446055948}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_626451977}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1749685941}

[[当去往同一目的地址存在多条等价路由时，设备在转发去往该目的地址的报文时，会在各条路径间实现负载分担；如果其中一条或者多条路径失效，所有业务流量会在剩余的可用路径间重新进行一次分配，实现新的负载均衡。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1749751477}

[[如果为了保持业务的连续性，需要保持在可用路径上转发的业务流量不改变转发路径，仅将故障路径上的原业务流量在可用路径上进行平均分配，可以配置等价路由增强模式功能。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1906788344}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1446121484}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1398228918}[使能]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv4]{lang="EN-US"}[等价路由增强模式功能。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1446187020}

[\[Sysname\] ecmp mode enhanced]{lang="EN-US"}

[The configuration will take effect at the next reboot. Continue? \[Y/N\]:y]{lang="EN-US"}

[Reboot device to make the configuration take effect.]{lang="EN-US"}

:::

[ ]{lang="EN-US"}

[**[fib lifetime]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1453104632}[命令用来配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv4/IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}[路由在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[FIB]{lang="EN-US"}[中的最大存活时间。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[undo fib lifetime]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x214284535}[命令用来恢复缺省情况。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x2026789788}

[**[fib]{lang="EN-US"}***[ ]{lang="EN-US"}***[lifetime]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *seconds*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x622315050}

[**[undo fib lifetime]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x586964160}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1595861064}

[[IPv4/IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_284279341}[路由在]{style="font-family:宋体"}[FIB]{lang="EN-US"}[中的最大存活时间为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[600]{lang="EN-US"}[秒。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_477151898}

[[RIB IPv4]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_954367492}[地址族视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}[/RIB IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}[地址族视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1850445517}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1496767877}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_739822830}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1659279885}

[*[seconds]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1118524807}[：]{style="font-family:宋体"}[FIB]{lang="SV"}[中路由的最大存活时间，取值范围为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="SV"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体;color:black"}[6000]{lang="SV"}[，单位为秒。取值为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="SV"}[时表示，协议或]{style="font-family:宋体"}[RIB]{lang="SV"}[进程倒换并重新恢复后，会立即通知]{style="font-family:宋体"}[FIB]{lang="SV"}[老化表项。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1514395374}

[[如果配置了该命令，协议在不配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[GR]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1964940519}[或]{style="font-family:宋体"}[NSR]{lang="EN-US"}[的情况下，协议或]{style="font-family:宋体"}[RIB]{lang="EN-US"}[倒换重新恢复后，会延迟上述配置时间间隔，再通知]{style="font-family:宋体"}[FIB]{lang="EN-US"}[老化表项，当配置为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[0]{lang="EN-US"}[时，会立刻通知]{style="font-family:宋体"}[FIB]{lang="EN-US"}[老化表项。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_954564100}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1846356522}[配置]{style="font-family:宋体"}[FIB]{lang="EN-US"}[中]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv4]{lang="EN-US"}[路由的最大存活时间为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[60]{lang="EN-US"}[秒。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[ ]{lang="EN-US"}

[**[ipv6 ecmp mode enhanced]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1445990412}[命令用来使能]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}[等价路由增强模式功能。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1446580236}

[**[undo ipv6 ecmp mode]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1446645772}

[[【缺省情况】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x989731396}

[[IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1446055947}[等价路由增强模式功能处于关闭状态。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_626910729}

[[系统视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1446121483}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1397901238}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_2127040817}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1446187019}

[[【使用指导】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1749751474}

[[当去往同一目的地址存在多条等价路由时，设备在转发去往该目的地址的报文时，会在各条路径间实现负载分担；如果其中一条或者多条路径失效，所有业务流量会在剩余的可用路径间重新进行一次分配，实现新的负载均衡。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1749817010}

[[如果为了保持业务的连续性，需要保持在可用路径上转发的业务流量不改变转发路径，仅将故障路径上的原业务流量在可用路径上进行平均分配，可以配置等价路由增强模式功能。]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1749358258}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1375929167}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1446252555}[使能]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}[等价路由增强模式功能。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> system-view]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1445793803}

[\[Sysname\] ipv6 ecmp mode enhanced]{lang="EN-US"}

[The configuration will take effect at the next reboot. Continue? \[Y/N\]:y]{lang="EN-US"}

[Reboot device to make the configuration take effect.]{lang="EN-US"}

------------------------------------------------------------------------

[**[reset ip routing-table statistics protocol]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1794678283}[命令用来清除路由表中的路由统计信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【命令】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1003551948}

[**[reset ip routing-table statistics protocol ]{lang="EN-US"}**[\[ **topology** *topo-name* \| **vpn-instance** *vpn-instance-name* \] { *protocol* \| **all** }]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_954629638}

[[【视图】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_954367495}

[[用户视图]{style="font-family:宋体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1850445516}

[[【缺省用户角色】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1496833413}

[[network-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_697170387}

[[mdc-admin]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x2111075565}

[[【参数】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1806397860}

[**[topology]{lang="EN-US"}**[ *topo-name*]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1749817008}[：清除指定拓扑的信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[topo-name]{lang="EN-US"}*[表示拓扑名，为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[31]{lang="EN-US"}[个字符的字符串，区分大小写；]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[base]{lang="EN-US"}**[为公网拓扑。如果未指定本参数，则清除公网的路由统计信息。本参数的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[vpn-instance ]{lang="EN-US"}***[vpn-instance-name]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1089584872}[：清除指定]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[的路由统计信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}*[vpn-instance-name]{lang="EN-US"}*[表示]{style="font-family:宋体"}[MPLS L3VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[的]{style="font-family:宋体"}[VPN]{lang="EN-US"}[实例名称，为]{style="font-family:宋体"}[1]{lang="EN-US"}[～]{style="font-family:宋体"}[31]{lang="EN-US"}[个字符的字符串，区分大小写。如果未指定本参数，则清除公网的路由统计信息。本参数的支持情况与设备的型号有关，请以设备的实际情况为准。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[*[protocol]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_954564103}[：清除]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv4]{lang="EN-US"}[路由表中指定路由协议的统计信息。目前可选择]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[bgp]{lang="EN-US"}**[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[direct]{lang="EN-US"}**[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[isis]{lang="EN-US"}**[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[ospf]{lang="EN-US"}**[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[rip]{lang="EN-US"}**[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[static]{lang="EN-US"}**[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[**[all]{lang="EN-US"}**]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1846356523}[：清除]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv4]{lang="EN-US"}[路由表中所有路由协议的统计信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[【举例】]{style="font-family:黑体"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_1120917348}

[[\# ]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_x1817002462}[清除路由表中的路由统计信息。]{style="font-family:宋体"}

[[\<Sysname\> reset ip routing-table statistics protocol all]{lang="EN-US"}]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_954629639}
:::

[*[protocol]{lang="EN-US"}*]{#struct_0_x4538_x8923_982796327}[：清除]{style="font-family:宋体"}[IPv6]{lang="EN-US"}[路由表中指定路由协议的统计信息。目前可选择]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[bgp4+]{lang="EN-US"}**[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[direct]{lang="EN-US"}**[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[isisv6]{lang="EN-US"}**[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[ospfv3]{lang="EN-US"}**[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[ripng]{lang="EN-US"}**[、]{style="font-family:宋体"}**[static]{lang="EN-US"}**[。]{style="font-family:宋体"}
